# marketplace digest 2026-11-18

> 本日报是**唯一完整的上架预告面**：下列 pass verdict 在本日报发布后进入成熟期（digest-appearance 门）。
> "no findings" ≠ safe.
> 📱 手机操作：点行的「审核单」链接开 issue → **Labels** 勾 `verdict:pass` 或 `verdict:concern` 即可写回裁决，零打字。
> 行标说明：⛔已被（包级/更新的）concern 刹车 = 该行已公告但不会放行；✅已被更新的 pass 解除 = concern 已被翻案；⚠️不在目录 = 该 verdict 针对的条目当前不在 index（已下架或未入库），公告照发但无槽可落。

| 177911-juling/Juling-Router | — | ai | 红旗在案，AI 判 concern：安装后脚本(postinstall)会执行自定义Node脚本并拉取远程目录数据，且应用捆绑的system_prompt.md中嵌有`curl ... \| bash`管道执行远程安装脚本的模式，二者结合构成安装时的远程代码执行/提示注入风险。 / red flags noted — AI concern: The postinstall runs custom Node scripts that fetch remote catalog data, and the bundled system-prompt markdown contains a `curl ... \| bash` pipe-to-bash pattern that downloads and executes a remote install script, together creating install-time remote-code-execution/prompt-injection risk. | [审核单](https://github.com/awesome-market/awesome-opencode-market/issues?q=is%3Aissue%20in%3Atitle%20%22%5Breview%5D%20177911-juling%2FJuling-Router%22) |
| 777genius/universal-agent-plugins-registry | — | ai | 红旗在案，AI 判 concern：已审内容（231/926）未见明确注入或外泄，但存在2个触及 .npmrc 的凭证读取红旗，且695个文件因超预算未进入评审并被明确标注为异常信号，仍需人工复查。 / red flags noted — AI concern: Reviewed content (231/926) shows no clear injection or exfiltration, but two credential-read redflags touch .npmrc paths and 695 files exceed the review budget as explicitly flagged anomalous, warranting manual review. | [审核单](https://github.com/awesome-market/awesome-opencode-market/issues?q=is%3Aissue%20in%3Atitle%20%22%5Breview%5D%20777genius%2Funiversal-agent-plugins-registry%22) |
| 777genius/universal-agent-plugins | — | ai | 红旗在案，AI 判 concern：包内含6.1MB与声明用途不符的Mach-O原生二进制、registry.ts中从agent-notifications.com执行curl\|bash、package.json postinstall含网络调用、多处base64解码为二进制、读取.npmrc凭据、Function构造函数混淆，且1720个文件未纳入评审 / red flags noted — AI concern: The package ships 6.1MB of Mach-O native binaries inconsistent with its declared purpose, executes a remote script via curl\|bash from agent-notifications.com in registry.ts, runs a postinstall network call, decodes base64 to binary in multiple files, reads .npmrc credentials, uses Function-constructor obfuscation, and leaves 1720 files unreviewed | [审核单](https://github.com/awesome-market/awesome-opencode-market/issues?q=is%3Aissue%20in%3Atitle%20%22%5Breview%5D%20777genius%2Funiversal-agent-plugins%22) |
| agustinusnathaniel/maestria | — | ai | 红旗在案，AI 判 concern：该包在 package.json 中配置了 prepare 脚本，安装时会自动执行未审计命令 `vp config`（已被扫描规则标记为红旗），且多处通过子进程 API 调用 shell；虽未发现明显的凭证外泄、网络外联、代码混淆或夸大/矛盾描述，但安装时执行任意命令本身值得警惕。 / red flags noted — AI concern: The package sets an install-time `prepare` script that auto-executes an unreviewed command (`vp config`) — flagged as a red flag — and shells out through child_process throughout, so while no clear credential exfiltration, network calls, obfuscation, or contradictory/misleading claims appear, running arbitrary commands during installation is worth flagging. | [审核单](https://github.com/awesome-market/awesome-opencode-market/issues?q=is%3Aissue%20in%3Atitle%20%22%5Breview%5D%20agustinusnathaniel%2Fmaestria%22) |
| alibaba/loongsuite-pilot | — | ai | 红旗在案，AI 判 concern：包内含无法文本审查的 native Mach-O 可执行二进制（0.9MB），且被二进制清单明确标记为“与声明用途不符”，同时存在读取/复制 .npmrc 与 .env 等凭证文件、并通过 curl\|bash 管道执行远程安装脚本的行为，存在未审查代码执行与凭证外泄风险。 / red flags noted — AI concern: The package bundles an unreviewable native Mach-O executable (0.9MB) explicitly flagged as inconsistent with its declared purpose, together with reading/copying .npmrc/.env credential files and executing remote installers via curl\|bash pipes, raising unreviewed-code-execution and credential-exfiltration concerns. | [审核单](https://github.com/awesome-market/awesome-opencode-market/issues?q=is%3Aissue%20in%3Atitle%20%22%5Breview%5D%20alibaba%2Floongsuite-pilot%22) |
| Alountk/Bloodbowl_Project | — | ai | 红旗在案，AI 判 concern：manifest描述为空且有224个文件未进入评审视野，均属于可疑信号，无法确认该包是否安全。 / red flags noted — AI concern: The manifest line provides an empty description and 224 files were excluded from review scope, both of which are suspicious signals that prevent confirming the package is safe. | [审核单](https://github.com/awesome-market/awesome-opencode-market/issues?q=is%3Aissue%20in%3Atitle%20%22%5Breview%5D%20Alountk%2FBloodbowl_Project%22) |
| alvarolorentedev/opencode-mobile | — | ai | 红旗在案，AI 判 concern：该包通过 new Function() 动态执行插件/GUI 代码，结合从服务器抓取任意 URL 内容、子进程派生以及一个超出预算未审文件，构成来自远端内容的任意代码执行风险。 / red flags noted — AI concern: The package dynamically executes arbitrary plugin/GUI code via the new Function() constructor, combined with fetching arbitrary URLs from the server and child-process spawning, which forms an arbitrary-code-execution vector from remote content. | [审核单](https://github.com/awesome-market/awesome-opencode-market/issues?q=is%3Aissue%20in%3Atitle%20%22%5Breview%5D%20alvarolorentedev%2Fopencode-mobile%22) |
| and-rs/dotfiles | — | ai | 清单描述“More dependencies !!!”为无意义占位符且扫描发现多个不可验证的.so二进制和execFile子进程调用，内容无法提取，无法确认描述与代码一致及二进制安全。 / The manifest description 'More dependencies !!!' is meaningless placeholder text while the scan flags multiple unverifiable .so binaries and child-process execFile usage, and package content failed extraction, so the description-to-code consistency and binary safety cannot be confirmed. | [审核单](https://github.com/awesome-market/awesome-opencode-market/issues?q=is%3Aissue%20in%3Atitle%20%22%5Breview%5D%20and-rs%2Fdotfiles%22) |
